# Commands · H · part 2

1,491 entries, harness-mem-wiki to help. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **harness-mem-wiki**: Build and refresh the workspace-level domain wiki from non-code sources — Jira, Confluence, SharePoint, NAS documents, NAS video — gated on each source actually being configured, synthesis-only, nothing ingested without confirmation. ([rbhattarai/se-harness](https://github.com/rbhattarai/se-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/se-harness-copilot/commands/harness-mem-wiki.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **harness-merge-train**: Multi-PR review/merge orchestration skill. Iterates a phase chain (M0-M9) over each PR — Clear judgment, rebase, pre-merge gates (Codex parallel + Pseudo CodeRabbit), push, CI wait, Real CodeRabbit Clear, Codex adversarial second opinion, squash merge, worktree cleanup, handoff sync — with fail-fast on any failure. Use when shipping 2+ open PRs in sequence, or when the user requests merge / merge-train / squash-multiple. ([ThomasEdwardYorke/cc-triad-relay](https://github.com/ThomasEdwardYorke/cc-triad-relay/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/commands/harness-merge-train.md) · ★1)
- **harness-methodology-openspec**: Install and initialize OpenSpec (Fission-AI/OpenSpec) for one repo or an entire se-harness workspace, gated on methodology:openspec actually being selected — setup only, /harness-goal drives the actual propose/archive lifecycle. ([rbhattarai/se-harness](https://github.com/rbhattarai/se-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/se-harness-copilot/commands/harness-methodology-openspec.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **harness-migrate**: 구버전 구조를 최신으로 마이그레이션 + 설치된 훅·스킬·규칙을 최신 템플릿으로 refresh — init은 기존 파일을 건너뛰므로 이 파일들의 템플릿 수정이 도달하는 유일한 경로 (AGENTS.md·CLAUDE.md 관리 절은 init이 렌더한다) ([bd-makers/team-harness](https://github.com/bd-makers/team-harness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-migrate.md) · ★1)
- **harness-next**: Start the next roadmap phase of TECHNICAL_PLAN.md as a governed Harness task. Usage: /harness-next ([Quattro-Commas/harness](https://github.com/Quattro-Commas/harness/blob/HEAD/.qoder/commands/harness-next.md))
- **harness-next**: Identify and implement the next task in PLAN.md ([RainMaker2020/HarnessLab](https://github.com/RainMaker2020/HarnessLab/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/harness-next.md))
- **harness-observe**: 관측 로그(.harness/observability) 스코어카드와 트립와이어 판정 — read-only, 발화 시 exit 1 ([bd-makers/team-harness](https://github.com/bd-makers/team-harness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-observe.md) · ★1)
- **harness-onboard**: Onboard an existing repository into the harness-engineering knowledge base and fill in gaps using an automated audit. ([lattice-technologies-inc/harness-engineering](https://github.com/lattice-technologies-inc/harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/harness-engineering/assets/commands/harness-onboard.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **harness-onboard**: Scan an existing project and generate an onboarding plan to bring it to full harness compliance. ([iVintik/codeharness](https://github.com/iVintik/codeharness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-onboard.md) · ★1)
- **harness-onboarding**: Generate a human-readable onboarding document from HARNESS.md, AGENTS.md, and REFLECTION_LOG.md — a friendly guide for new team members ([Habitat-Thinking/ai-literacy-superpowers](https://github.com/Habitat-Thinking/ai-literacy-superpowers/blob/HEAD/ai-literacy-superpowers/commands/harness-onboarding.md) · ★124 · 3 copies)
- **harness-operate**: Full production operations workflow — KPI dashboard, reliability scan, cost review, improvement planning, knowledge extraction, decision pattern matching, and TK-to-instruction conversion. Use when an agent is live and needs a weekly/monthly operational review, or when running any individual operations step. Use --mode post-retro for closed-loop retrospective: original Evidence Gate hypothesis vs actual metrics. ([kimsanguine/hplan](https://github.com/kimsanguine/hplan/blob/HEAD/hplan/commands/harness-operate.md) · ★3)
- **harness-opportunities**: [Code Quality] Use when a workflow step or the user asks for harness opportunities in a change. Classifies the diff and identifies worthwhile deterministic guards or reusable lessons. ([duc01226/easy-claude](https://github.com/duc01226/easy-claude/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/harness-opportunities.md) · ★3)
- **harness-optimize**: 하네스 최적화 — 토큰 비용 절감, 캐시 히트율 향상, 응답 속도 개선을 위한 진단 및 튜닝 ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-optimize.md) · ★1)
- **harness-parallel**: Run the parallel Auto-Harness orchestration flow with worktree workers. /auto-harness:harness-parallel &lt;brief or clarification/spec-approval reply&gt; ([redker56/auto-harness](https://github.com/redker56/auto-harness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-parallel.md) · ★15)
- **harness-pass**: Pasada completa del harness — ejecuta todos los verificadores formales (citas, prosa↔JSON, hashes, deuda, auto-indulgencia, doc↔config) y muestra reporte honesto. ([stevenvo780/EstructurasPreontologicas](https://github.com/stevenvo780/EstructurasPreontologicas/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/harness-pass.md))
- **harness-patch**: JIT 即时诊断与补丁工作流：分析刚才的运行偏差，生成候选补丁，支持应用与回滚 ([LUAgam/stage-harness](https://github.com/LUAgam/stage-harness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-patch.md) · ★1)
- **harness-pinit**: 신규 프로젝트를 하네스 스킬 파이프라인을 통해 역추적해 분석하고 .claude/ 초기 설정을 제안한다. 빌드 파일 → 기술 스택 → 활성 스킬 → 검증 커버리지 → 누락 자산 순으로 보고서 생성. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/commands/harness-pinit.md) · ★1)
- **harness-plan**: 你是 **Planner（規劃官）**，負責將使用者的需求轉化為結構化的 Plans.md 任務。 ([twtrubiks/mongo-fastapi-svelte-chat](https://github.com/twtrubiks/mongo-fastapi-svelte-chat/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/harness-plan.md) · ★6)
- **harness-plan**: Full technical architecture and strategy planning — orchestration pattern, 3-tier design, memory architecture, model routing, architecture doc, and strategy review. ([kimsanguine/hplan](https://github.com/kimsanguine/hplan/blob/HEAD/hplan/commands/harness-plan.md) · ★3)

Use when user mentions: create a plan, add tasks, update Plans.md, sync status, check progress, show principles, /harness-plan, /plan-with-agent, /sync-status, /principles. Do NOT load for: implementation, code review, or release tasks. - **harness:acceptance-eval**: Pre-execution LLM-judgment skill: does a spec carry measurable, testable, complete acceptance criteria before work begins? Resolves the spec's acceptance section, critiques observability / testability / completeness (advisory), flags user-visible behaviors with no covering test (advisory), and emits a confidence-rated AcceptanceVerdict (MEASURABLE | NOT_MEASURABLE | INCONCLUSIVE). Authority is derived in TypeScript, never from the LLM: a high-confidence NOT_MEASURABLE blocks merge; every other verdict is advisory. The upstream twin of the outcome-eval ship gate. ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/commands/acceptance-eval.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
